Let's design local search algorithms:

A. Continuous coordinate descent with sum constraint via **pair moves**.
We can use a compensation pair: For each variable i, pick j = (i+1)%N? Or several. Candidate new_ivalues: 0,1, old +/- eps, maybe old ±0.1. New_j = old_i+old_j - new_i. Need both within [0,1]. If not, skip. Update two coordinates. To update c for two coordinates, we can simply apply coordinate update for i, then for j sequentially (using current h). But second update coefficients use h after first; that's correct if we change variables one by one, preserving sum after second. Need ensure final sum exactly. If applying i and then j, intermediate sum drifts but c updates additive linear; okay. Cost per pair trial 2*O(N). Candidate set maybe 9 values. For N=100, 900 pairs *2*100=180k vector ops/pass. Good.
But pair moves may be too restrictive; many variables need global sum adjustment. We can alternate with **global projection**: after a pass, repair sum by line search on all variables: h_new = h + δ*(1-2h)? To adjust sum, choose direction v = (1-h) - h =1-2h? If sum needs increase, add t*(1-h) scaled; if decrease, subtract t*h. This moves all variables toward 1 or 0, preserving bounds for t chosen. It may significantly change c; we can accept if improves. This is a global coordinate step to enforce sum. Since pair moves preserve sum, initial sum okay. But SLSQP/other may leave errors.

Alternatively use **cyclic pairs**: partition variables into pairs, optimize each pair jointly: For pair (i,j), their sum s fixed. Let a_i in [max(0,s-1), min(1,s)], h_j=s-a. The objective as function a is max of quadratics/linear; we can try grid values for a (e.g., 21) and update both. This explores fractions within pair while preserving sum. This may smooth. We can implement pair coordinate descent by trying candidate a_i values for each pair. For each trial, apply changes to i and j. Candidate a values: current, boundaries, 0,1, maybe linearly spaced 0..1 clipped. Number pairs N/2; candidates ~11; cost higher but okay for N=100 (50*11*2*100=110k ops/pass). 50 passes 5.5M, fine. This handles sum robustly.

B. Binary swap local search:
For even N, binary h with M=N/2. Move: choose one 1 position p and one 0 position q; swap values. This preserves sum. Evaluate new c by updates for p and q. We can hill climb: for each pass, try all possible swaps? Number ones*zeros ~2500 for N=100. Each swap O(N) vector ->250k ops/pass. 50 passes 12.5M, okay. But trying all swaps repeatedly expensive but manageable. We can improve by using steepest descent: For each pass, compute best swap? 2500*100=250k per pass, 100 passes 25M vector ops (length 100) maybe okay in Python if optimized? Each update includes vector operations N, 2500*2 updates ~5000 vector length 100 =500k per pass, 100 passes=50M floats, okay. Could do.
But local continuous may be better. We can first binary-optimize using swaps to local optimum, then continuous pair polish.

C. Greedy construction: Start from random, then use steepest coordinate/pair descent.

D. Use simulated annealing for binary: random swaps accept worse to escape local optima. Time.

E. Use scipy SLSQP on objective `softmax` or `max` with gradient. We can implement vectorized c from scratch for SLSQP; N=100, evaluations maybe 200, each O(N^2) loop if using Python for d (100 dot products) =10k operations, trivial. Actually 200*10k=2M, fine. SLSQP with N=100 variables and 100 nonlinear? It only uses objective and constraint; each iteration many function evals maybe 500. Fine. We can use `method='SLSQP'`, bounds, equality sum. Objective: max c *2/N. For nonsmooth, SLSQP approximates gradient finite diff, may struggle. Provide analytic gradient of smooth surrogate? We can let SLSQP use central differences with high dim (100) costly: each gradient ~200 evals. Too expensive if 100 iterations (20k evals*10k=200M, okay maybe but SLSQP overhead). Better provide gradient for a smooth objective: e.g., C_smooth = (1/eps) logsumexp(eps*c) *dx? Derivative wrt h_i can be computed from weights w_l = exp(eps*c_l)/sum. c_l are bilinear; derivative = w_y * dc/dh. We can implement SLSQP with analytic gradient (method supports jac). Constraint gradient ones. This is powerful.
But logsumexp smooth over infeasible? Max of c terms; weights concentrated near max. We can also use active max objective: use `np.max(c)` and gradient of active set (where c>max-tol). SLSQP may handle nonsmooth finite diff but analytic active gradient better. We can define objective as `max(c) * dx + small * variance?` and jac from active terms. Let's outline code structure:
```
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def correlation_array(h):
    N=len(h)
    offset=N-1
    c=np.zeros(2*N-1)
    one_minus = 1.0-h
    for d in range(N):
        L=N-d
        c[offset+d] = float(np.dot(h[:L], one_minus[d:]))
        c[offset-d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], one_minus[:L]))
    return c

def objective_c5(h):
    N=len(h); dx=2.0/N
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    c=np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))
    return float(c*dx)
```
But for local search use manual c.

Repair:
```
def repair_sum(h, n=None):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True)
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=(len(h)/2.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        err=target-h.sum()
        if abs(err)<1e-13: break
        if err>0:
            mask=h<1.0
        else:
            mask=h>0.0
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        add=err/np.sum(mask)
        if abs(add) >= 1.0: add=np.sign(err)*(1.0) #? Better distribute unit?
        h[mask]+=add
        np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    # final exact adjust
    err=target-h.sum()
    if abs(err)>1e-12:
       # choose index with max slack direction and within feasible
       if err>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1.0-h)
       else: idx=np.argmin(h)
       h[idx]+=err
       h[idx]=min(1,max(0,h[idx]))
       # if still err due clip, loop
    return h
```
But if err cannot fit in one, distribution needed. Better robust:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h):
  target=len(h)/2.0
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  for _ in range(100):
    err=target-h.sum()
    if abs(err)<=1e-12: break
    if err>0:
       slack=1.0-h
       idxs=np.argsort(-slack)
    else:
       slack=h
       idxs=np.argsort(slack)
    # add small chunk to first few until err consumed
    step=0.01
    rem=err
    for idx in idxs:
       if rem==0: break
       if rem>0: amt=min(1-h[idx], rem)
       else: amt=min(h[idx], -rem)
       h[idx]+=amt; rem-=amt
       if abs(rem)<1e-14: break
  # If still, set first feasible by exact
```
This is O(N log N) but fine.

```
def to_binary(v):
  n=len(v); target=n//2
  order=np.argsort(-v)
  b=np.zeros(n)
  b[order[:target]]=1.0
  return b
```
If n odd target? We only binary even. For parent n=95, resample even and binary.

    h = np.array(h, float, copy=True)
    c = correlation_array(h)  # or passed
    offset=n-1; best_m=np.max(c); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    steps = [0.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.75,0.2,0.8]
    no_improve=0
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       improved=False
       for i in range(n//2): # pairs (i, n//2+i) maybe random pairs
          j = (i + n//2) % n
          s = h[i]+h[j]
          # candidate values for x=h_i
          curr=h[i]
          cands = [curr, 0.0, 1.0, s-1.0, s.0? boundaries, random?]
          # also fractional grid around curr: curr +/- 0.05,0.1,0.25

`apply_change(c, h, idx, new)`:
```
def apply_change(c,h,idx,new):
    old=h[idx]
    delta=new-old
    if abs(delta)<1e-15: return c
    J=global ARANGE? Pass J to avoid allocate.
    off=len(h)-1
    pos = off + (J - idx)
    neg = off + (idx - J)
    c[pos] += delta * (1.0 - h)  # h contains old values (idx old)
    c[neg] -= delta * h
    c[off] -= delta*delta
    h[idx] = new
    return c
```

Candidate values generation top-level `pair_candidates(h, i, j)`:
```
def pair_candidates(h,i,j):
  s=h[i]+h[j]
  lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s)
  curr=h[i]
  vals = [curr, 0.0, 1.0, lo, hi, (lo+hi)/2, s-0.5, s/2]
  # perturb: curr ± [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.25,0.5]
  for d in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.25,0.5): add curr±d
  # maybe values to make y 0/1/(0.5)
  vals += [s, s-1, 0.5, s-0.5]
  # unique within bounds
```

Trial swap with in-place original h and c_trial:
```
def swap_trial(c, h, p, q, pos, neg, offset):
    c_trial=c.copy()
    oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]
    # apply p change to 0: if oldp not 0
    apply_change_with_idx(c_trial,h,p,0.0,...) # this modifies h[p]
    apply_change_with_idx(c_trial,h,q,1.0,...) # h[p]=0, h[q]=oldq still old
    m=np.max(c_trial)
    h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq
    return m
```
But apply_change for q uses h[p]=0 as coefficient for pair (p,q), which is correct for new state. For pair (q,p), h[p] new. Good. h[q] set after first? apply_change sets h[q]=1 at end; before that h[q]=oldq. During q update, coefficients for other variables include h[p]=0 and h[q]=oldq at diagonal? Diagonal subtraction uses delta_q and old's h[q]; correct. For off-diag pair (p,q) as second? In q's first term `c[pos[q]] += delta_q*(1-h)`: for j=p, coefficient (1-h[p]) uses h[p]=0 new; correct. For q's second term `c[neg[q]] -= delta_q*h`: for j=p, coefficient h[p]=0 new; correct. Good.

Continuous pair trial with pair (i,j):
```
def pair_trial(c,h,i,j,x,y,pos,neg,offset):
   c_trial=c.copy(); old_i=h[i]; old_j=h[j]
   apply_change_with_idx(c_trial,h,i,x,...)
   apply_change_with_idx(c_trial,h,j,y,...)
   m=max(c_trial)
   h[i]=old_i; h[j]=old_j
   return m, c_trial
```

def binary_anneal(h,c,time_limit,seed):
   n, idx_struct=...
   cur_m=max(c); best_m=... best_h...
   ones=zeros...
   # Initial temperature maybe max_m - avg? Use T=0.3 count; cool.
   T=1.0
   iteration=0

      # choose random swap
      p=random ones, q=random zeros
      m=swap_trial
      if m <= cur_m or random()<exp((cur_m-m)/T): accept if m<cur_m+T

```

Could formulate as minimizing maximum c via **Linear Programming over h if we relax product by McCormick**. For fixed L, constraints c_δ≤L, bilinear. McCormick with h∈[0,1] and products h_i h_j introduces z≤h_i, z≤h_j, z≥h_i+h_j-1, z≥0. Constraint ∑ h_i - ∑ z_{i,i+δ} ≤ L. This is a mixed-integer? If h continuous, McCormick gives convex relaxation but with constraints for all pairs (i,j) and cδ terms (positive δ). But variables h continuous, no integer. The feasible region of continuous h with bilinear constraints is nonconvex; McCormick relaxation may be solvable as LP? Let's see: We minimize λ s.t. for each δ>0 and δ<0? cδ=∑(h_i - h_i h_{i+δ})≤λ. We need variables p_{iδ}=h_i h_{i+δ}. McCormick: p≤h_i, p≤h_{i+δ}, p≥h_i+h_{i+δ}-1, p≥0. Constraint ∑h_i over valid - ∑p ≤λ. This is LP! Because p variables linear, constraints linear. However the relaxation allows p less than product? McCormick for given h bounds gives p in [max(0,h_i+h_j-1), min(h_i,h_j)]; product lies in box. Minimizing λ with -∑p constraint would push p to maximum allowed (min) to reduce c? Wait constraint ∑h - ∑p ≤λ, so to make feasible for smaller λ, need p as large as possible. McCormick upper bound p≤min(h_i,h_j); setting p=min may overstate product (if h_i≠h_j <1) and make problem easier, not valid lower. But for finding feasible h, if LP solution has p equal actual product (when h binary or h_i=h_j), it's feasible. Could use LP to guide? CVXPY LP with ~N^2 variables (10k) maybe heavy but solvable. Index arrays:
```
def build_idx(n):
    J = np.arange(n)
    off = n - 1
    pos = [off + (J - i) for i in range(n)]
    neg = [off + (i - J) for i in range(n)]
    return (n, off, J, pos, neg)
```
Memory list of arrays. For n=128, 128 arrays*128 =16k ints, okay. For multiple N, build cache dict? In run, can cache.

Apply change:
```
def apply_change(c, h, idx, new, off, pos, neg):
    old = h[idx]
    delta = new - old
    if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
        return
    c[pos[idx]] += delta * (1.0 - h)
    c[neg[idx]] -= delta * h
    c[off] -= delta * delta
    h[idx] = new
```
Need `pos` and `neg` lists; `pos[idx]` array. `h` should be old for idx before set; diagonal correction exact. Check diagonal with old=0.5, new=0.6 delta0.1: add pos:0.1*(1-0.5)=0.05; neg: -0.05; net diag 0; subtract delta^2=0.01 -> correct? Diagonal change new(1-new)-old(1-old)=0.24-0.25=-0.01. Good.

Pair trial:
```
def pair_trial(c, h, i, j, xi, yj, off, pos, neg):
    c2 = c.copy()
    old_i = h[i]; old_j = h[j]
    apply_change(c2, h, i, xi, off, pos, neg)
    apply_change(c2, h, j, yj, off, pos, neg)
    m = float(np.max(c2))
    h[i] = old_i; h[j] = old_j
    return m, c2
```
But apply_change modifies h[i] then h[j]; when applying j, h[i]=xi, good. Restores both. If i==j? We won't call.
Potential issue: If pair move keeps sum: xi+yj == old_i+old_j. Due floating, maybe; use `yj = (old_i+old_j) - xi` to enforce.

Pair candidates top-level:
```
def pair_candidate_values(h, i, j):
    s = h[i] + h[j]
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    curr = h[i]
    vals = [curr, 0.0, 1.0, lo, hi, (lo+hi)*0.5, s - 0.5, s*0.5]
    for d in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
        vals.append(curr+d); vals.append(curr-d)
    vals.append(0.5)
    # maybe random? Could pass rng but function top-level; use deterministic plus random inside pair_descent.
    # clip/filter unique
    res=[]; seen=set()
    for v in vals:
       if v<lo-1e-10 or v>hi+1e-10: continue
       v= min(hi,max(lo,v))
       key=round(v,10)
       if key not in seen: res.append(v); seen.add(key)
    return res
```
In pair_descent, also generate a few random values from uniform lo-hi if rng provided. Need pass rng to function? Top-level can accept rng, pair_candidate_values can take rng? To avoid function with rng optional. We'll generate randoms inside pair_descent and add to list.

`swap_trial` top-level:
```
def swap_trial(c,h,p,q,off,pos,neg):
    c2=c.copy()
    oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]
    apply_change(c2,h,p,0.0,off,pos,neg)
    apply_change(c2,h,q,1.0,off,pos,neg)
    m=float(np.max(c2))
    h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq
    return m,c2
```
If p is not 1 or q not0? We only use. If p/q old maybe due continuous? Ensure binary before.

SLSQP polish top-level:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, idx, deadline, maxiter=80, active_tol=0.1):
    n=len(h)
    if time.time() > deadline: return h, c, m
    target=n/2.0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res = minimize(slsqp_fun, h, args=(n,2.0/n,active_tol), jac=slsqp_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'args':(target,)}], method='SLSQP', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-10, 'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       return h,c,inf
    x=res.x
    # repair
    x=repair_sum(x)
    c=correlation_array(x); m=max(c)*2/n
    return x,c,m
```

Function `make_resampled(p,N,linear=True)`:
```
def resample_parent(p,N,linear):
   if p is None: return None
   np_=len(p)
   xnew=(np.arange(N)+0.5)*2.0/N
   xold=(np.arange(np_)+0.5)*2.0/np_
   if linear: v=np.interp(xnew,xold,p, left=p[0], right=p[-1])
   else: idx=np.clip((xnew*0.5*np_).astype(int),0,np_-1); v=p[idx]
   return repair_sum(v)
```
Parent can be fractional. Nearest step might preserve binary structure. Both.

`make_binary_from(v)`: for even N:
```
def make_binary(v):
  n=len(v); target=n//2
  order=np.argsort(-v)
  b=np.zeros(n); b[order[:target]]=1; return b
```
If v length odd? target floor? We'll use even.

Detailed helper implementations:

Repair sum:
```
def repair_sum(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = h.size / 2.0
    for _ in range(50):
        err = target - h.sum()
        if abs(err) < 1e-13:
            break
        if err > 0:
            # add to variables with most slack, in small chunks
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
            rem = err
            for idx in order:
                if rem <= 0: break
                amt = min(1.0 - h[idx], rem)
                h[idx] += amt
                rem -= amt
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            rem = -err
            for idx in order:
                if rem <= 0: break
                amt = min(h[idx], rem)
                h[idx] -= amt
                rem -= amt
    # final adjustment in big steps if still error due arg sort? use while
    for _ in range(10):
        err = target - h.sum()
        if abs(err) < 1e-12:
            break
        if err > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + err)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + err)
    # If still (shouldn't), uniform
    err = target - h.sum()
    if abs(err) > 1e-9:
        h += err / h.size
        np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```
Sorting each iteration O(NlogN) but repair few times. Could optimize uniform distribute first:
```
h += err/h.size; clip; then residual fix. Simpler and robust:
for _ in range(20):
 err=target-sum
 if abs<1e-12 break
 mask = h<1 if err>0 else h>0
 if not any: break
 h[mask] += err/mask.sum()
 clip
```
Then final exact by adjusting one with slack. Let's use this simpler.
```
def repair_sum(h):
 h=np.array(h,float,copy=True).ravel()
 np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 target=len(h)/2.0
 for _ in range(30):
   err=target-h.sum()
   if abs(err)<1e-12: break
   if err>0:
      mask=h<1.0
   else:
      mask=h>0.0
   if not np.any(mask): break
   # if err/mask sum > max slack? add in steps
   add=err/np.count_nonzero(mask)
   h[mask]+=add
   np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
# exact residual
for _ in range(10):
  err=target-h.sum()
  if abs(err)<1e-13: break
  if err>0:
     idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
     h[idx]=min(1.0, h[idx]+err)
  else:
     idx=int(np.argmin(h))
     h[idx]=max(0.0, h[idx]+err)
# final if still (rare) distribute to all with slack exact
err=target-h.sum();
if abs(err)>1e-12:
  if err>0:
     slack=1-h; s=sum(slack)
  else: slack=h; s=sum(h)
  if s>0: h += (err/s)* (slack if err>0 else -h?) This gets messy. Assume okay.
return h
```
If h all 1 and need decrease, mask h>0 works. Uniform add may overshoot all to 1 if error large; loop and clip repeated. If error large and many at bounds, residual may remain; exact adjust picks with slack. Good.

`evaluate_candidate`:
```
def evaluate_candidate(h):
    try:
       h=repair_sum(h)
    except Exception:
       return None, 1e100
    if h.size==0 or np.any(h<-1e-9) or np.any(h>1+1e-9):
       return None, 1e100
    c5 = objective_c5(h)
    if not np.isfinite(c5): return h,1e100
    return h,c5
```
`objective_c5`:
```
def objective_c5(h):
    n=h.size; dx=2.0/n
    v=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(v)*dx)
```

`correlation_array` uses manual; for speed, could use `np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')` which returns reversed but max same. For local c array, order matters for gradient? Gradient would need map to numpy lags. We can use manual for consistency and gradient. Use loops; for n up to128, 128 dot products. SLSQP might call many times; loops okay. Could use `np.correlate` and reverse c? For gradient manual easier. Use manual.
```
def correlation_array(h):
  n=len(h); off=n-1; L=2*n-1; c=np.zeros(L)
  om=1.0-h
  for d in range(n):
     m=n-d
     c[off+d] = float(np.dot(h[:m], om[d:]))
     c[off-d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], om[:m]))
  return c
```
For n=95, 95 dots; fine.

def build_idx(n):
  J=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
  off=n-1
  pos=[(off+J-i).astype(int) for i in range(n)]
  neg=[(off+J-i).astype(int)? wait neg=off+i-J.
  return n,off,J,pos,neg
```

`apply_change`: Ensure `c` and `h` are numpy arrays. `pos[idx]` list of arrays.
```
def apply_change(c, h, idx, new, off, pos, neg):
    old=float(h[idx])
    delta=new-old
    if abs(delta)<1e-16: return
    p=pos[idx]; q=neg[idx]
    c[p] += delta * (1.0 - h)
    c[q] -= delta * h
    c[off] -= delta*delta
    h[idx]=new
```
Potential issue: When c[p] and c[q] refer to same array view? They are separate int arrays; c advanced indexing creates copy? For `c[p] +=` with integer array, it does buffering and works. If p and q overlap (many), two separate operations. Good. But `c[p] += delta*(1-h)` computes RHS then adds; no in-place shared issues. `c[off]` scalar after. We subtract delta^2 at end to fix diagonal; if c[off] also updated in p and q, order fine.

Pair candidate values: Need pass `lo, hi` maybe to avoid recompute. We'll compute inside pair des. Use deterministic list.
```
def pair_candidates(h,i,j):
    s=float(h[i]+h[j]); lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s); curr=float(h[i])
    vals=[curr,0.0,1.0,lo,hi,0.5,s-0.5,0.5*s]
    perturb=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35)
    for d in perturb:
       vals.append(curr+d); vals.append(curr-d)
    # Also if s==1 binary, boundaries already.
    res=[]; seen=set()
    for v in vals:
       if not np.isfinite(v): continue
       if v<lo-1e-9 or v>hi+1e-9: continue
       if v<lo: v=lo
       elif v>hi: v=hi
       key=round(float(v),10)
       if key not in seen:
          seen.add(key); res.append(float(v))
    return res
```

Pair descent:
```
def pair_descent(h, c, idx, deadline, max_passes=50, seed=0, random_candidates=2):
    n,off,J,pos,neg = idx
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    c = np.array(c, dtype=float, copy=True)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    w = (1 << 32) # random pair order? Use permutation
    passes=0
    while passes < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
       # choose random permutation of indices, pair adjacents
       perm = rng.permutation(n)
       improved_pass=False
       for t in range(n//2):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          i=int(perm[2*t]); j=int(perm[2*t+1])
          s=float(h[i]+h[j]); lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s)
          if hi-lo < 1e-12: continue
          vals=pair_candidates(h,i,j)
          # add random
          if random_candidates>0:
             for _ in range(random_candidates): vals.append(float(lo + rng.random()*(hi-lo)))
          local_best_m=cur_m; local_best_c=None; local_xi=None
          for xi in vals:
             yj=s-xi
             # quick feasibility
             m,c2=pair_trial(c,h,i,j,float(xi),float(yj),off,pos,neg)
             if m < local_best_m - 1e-12:
                local_best_m=m; local_best_c=c2; local_xi=xi
          if local_best_c is not None:
             # apply to h/c (c2 already corresponds)
             c=local_best_c
             # set h values; note c2 built from temporary h with original i,j, h restored. Set
             h[i]=float(local_xi); h[j]=float(s-local_xi)
             cur_m=local_best_m
             if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_m=cur_m; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
             improved_pass=True
       passes += 1
       if improved_pass:
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve >= 3: break
    return best_h,best_c,best_m
```
Potential issue: We assign `c=local_best_c`; local_best_c is a copy. `h` updated. Later `pair_trial` uses current c,h. Good.
No global best record within; run records after return.

`slsqp` functions:
```
def slsqp_fun(x, n, dx, active_tol):
   c=correlation_array(x)
   return float(np.max(c)*dx)
def slsqp_jac(x, n, dx, active_tol):
   c=correlation_array(x)
   m=np.max(c)
   lags=np.where(c >= m - active_tol)[0]
   if lags.size == 0: lags=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   off=n-1
   for li in lags:
      d=int(li)-off
      start=max(0, -d)
      end=min(n, n-d)
      if start>=end: continue
      first=np.arange(start,end)
      second=first+d
      grad[first] += (1.0 - x[second])
      grad[second] -= x[first]
   grad /= float(lags.size)
   grad *= dx
   return grad
```
For d=0, first=second; grad += (1-x)-x =1-2x, correct. For d negative, first start=-d (positive), second=first+d (0..), correct. For d positive, first 0, second positive. Good.
`sum_constraint(x,target)`.
`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, idx, deadline, maxiter=60, active_tol=0.1):
   n=len(h); target=n/2.0
   h=repair_sum(h)
   if time.time() > deadline: return h, correlation_array(h), max...
   dx=2.0/n
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
   try:
      res=minimize(slsqp_fun, h.copy(), args=(n,dx,active_tol), jac=slsqp_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'args':(target,)}], method='SLSQP', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10,'disp':False})
   except Exception:
      return h, correlation_array(h), float(np.max(correlation_array(h))*dx)
   x=repair_sum(res.x)
   c=correlation_array(x); m=float(np.max(c)*dx)
   return x,c,m
```
Note `idx` unused; can remove.
SLSQP may be slow. Could use `maxiter=30` for time.

def optimize_candidate(h_in, idx, alloc_s, seed, deadline, force_binary=False):
    n=idx[0]
    h=repair_sum(h_in)

    if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h,best_c,best_m
    is_bin=bool(np.all((h<1e-6)|(h>1.0-1e-6)))

       t_bin=min(deadline, time.time()+0.35*t_left)

In `swap_trial`, after applying p then q, h[p]=0,h[q]=1, then restore oldp,oldq. Good.
```
def swap_trial(c,h,p,q,off,pos,neg):
    c2=c.copy()
    oldp=float(h[p]); oldq=float(h[q])
    apply_change(c2,h,p,0.0,off,pos,neg)
    apply_change(c2,h,q,1.0,off,pos,neg)
    m=float(np.max(c2))
    h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq
    return m,c2
```
If oldp already 0 or oldq 1 due not binary, still okay but search assumes.

`binary_search` implementation details:
```
def binary_search(h_in,c_in,idx,deadline,seed,full_sample=5000,max_hill_passes=30,sa_min_iters=20000):
    n,off,J,pos,neg=idx
    h=np.array(h_in,float,copy=True); c=np.array(c_in,float,copy=True)
    # snap binary
    h = np.where(h>0.5,1.0,0.0)
    # repair sum? if target not reached due threshold? make_binary ensures. But if input continuous target n/2, threshold top N/2; sum exact. If not, adjust? Use if sum != n/2: sort etc.
    target=n/2.0; if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-9: h=make_binary(h)
    cur_m=float(np.max(c))
    # Need recompute c after snapping maybe if input not exact? Better recompute:
    c=correlation_array(h); cur_m=float(np.max(c))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_m=cur_m; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    no_improve=0
    passes=0
    while passes<max_hill_passes and time.time()<deadline:
       ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
       if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
       total=int(len(ones)*len(zeros))
       local_best=cur_m; best_c2=None; best_pq=None
       sampled=0
       if total <= full_sample:
           for ip in range(len(ones)):
              p=int(ones[ip])
              for iq in range(len(zeros)):
                 q=int(zeros[iq])
                 m,c2=swap_trial(c,h,p,q,off,pos,neg)
                 if m < local_best - 1e-12:
                    local_best=m; best_c2=c2; best_pq=(p,q)
                 sampled+=1
       else:
           # sample unique maybe not necessary
           for _ in range(full_sample):
              p=int(ones[rng.integers(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.integers(len(zeros))])
              m,c2=swap_trial(...)
              if m<... 
       if best_pq is not None and local_best < cur_m -1e-12:
           p,q=best_pq
           h[p]=0.0; h[q]=1.0; c=best_c2; cur_m=local_best
           if cur_m < best_m -1e-12: best... 
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve>=2: break
       passes+=1
       # occasionally recompute c? after accept maybe if passes%10==0: c=correlation_array(h); cur_m=max(c)
    # SA from current (or best if no improvement)
    if time.time()<deadline:
       # Start from current local optimum; set cur to best if current worsened? Use best.
       # If no hill improvement, current=best.
       # Temperature schedule
       T=0.8; it=0; min_T=0.05
       while time.time()<deadline and T>min_T:
          it+=1
          ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
          if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
          p=int(ones[rng.integers(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.integers(len(zeros))])
          m,c2=swap_trial(...)
          if m < cur_m or (m <= cur_m + T and rng.random() < math.exp(-(m-cur_m)/T)):
             h[p]=0.0; h[q]=1.0; c=c2; cur_m=m
             if m < best_m -1e-12:
                best_m=m; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); T=max(T,0.4)? # if improved, keep temp
          # if current significantly worse than best, reset
          if it % 20000 == 0:
             if cur_m > best_m + 5.0:
                h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); cur_m=best_m
             T=max(min_T, T*0.99997)
          else:
             T=max(min_T, T*0.99999)
    return best_h,best_c,best_m
```
The cooling factor per iteration: 0.99999 for 100k ->0.905, not much; 0.99995 ->0.6. Fine. Use time-based? Simpler: `T = max(min_T, 0.8 * (remaining_time / total_sa_time))` needs total. Use iteration count up to e.g. 200000: `T=0.8*(1 - 0.99*it/200000)+0.05`. But time limit variable. Simpler exponential.

def improve_candidate(h_in, idx, time_budget, seed, deadline, do_binary, do_slsqp):
    n=idx[0]; best_h=None; best_count=1e100
    h0=repair_sum(h_in)

    m0=float(np.max(c0)*2/n)
    # record base internally by updating best? Return, but intermediate not to run.
    # binary

       h_bin=make_binary(h0)
       deadline_bin=min(deadline, time.time()+0.35*time_budget)
       hb,cb,mb=binary_search(h_bin, correlation_array(h_bin), idx, deadline_bin, seed)
       mb5=mb*2/n; update
    # pair from current (if binary used use hb else h0)
    if time.time()<deadline:
       h_start = hb if do_binary and ... else h0
       c_start = cb if ... else c0
       deadline_pair=min(deadline, time.time()+0.35*time_budget)
       hp,cp,mp=pair_descent(h_start,c_start,idx,deadline_pair,seed+1000)
       update
    # slsqp from best_h? Need best_h local. We can return best_h,best_c,best_count; run records. Also maybe SLSQP can use start best_h local.

       update
       if time<deadline: active_tol=0.4 maxiter lower
    return best_h,best_c,best_count

Let's create top-level `is_binary_like(h)`:
```
def is_binary_like(h):
   n=len(h)
   if n==0: return False
   frac = np.sum((h > 1e-7) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-7))
   return frac <= max(3, 0.3*n)
```

Let's define `resample_parent` robust for p length maybe 0.
```
def resample_parent(p,N,linear=True):
    if p is None or len(p)==0: return np.full(N,0.5)
    np_=len(p)
    xnew=(np.arange(N)+0.5)*(2.0/N)
    xold=(np.arange(np_)+0.5)*(2.0/np_)
    if linear:
       v=np.interp(xnew, xold, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    else:
       idx=np.floor(xnew*(0.5*np_)).astype(int)
       idx=np.clip(idx,0,np_-1)
       v=p[idx]
    return repair_sum(v)
```
For step parent, xnew*0.5*np_ = center scaled by Np/N. Good.
If parent sampling was left endpoints, using centers okay.

`make_binary`:
```
def make_binary(v):
    n=len(v)
    if n%2==0:
       target=n//2
    else:
       target=int(round(n/2.0))
    order=np.argsort(-v)
    b=np.zeros(n)
    b[order[:target]]=1.0
    # If target*? sum not n/2 for odd; repair? We mostly use even.
    return b
```
For even sum exact.

def sine_seed(n, seed, a=0.5, freq=0.73, phase=0.0):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   freq = rng.choice([0.7,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.3])? No top-level can.
   phase = rng.random()*2*math.pi
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
   v=0.5 + a*np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*x + phase)
   v=np.clip(v,0,1)
   return repair_sum(v)
```

Let's implement `add_candidate(cand_list, hcand, N, limit=16)`:
```
def add_candidate(cand_list, hcand, N, limit=16):
    hrep,c = evaluate_candidate(hcand)
    if hrep is None: return None, 1e100
    cand_list.append((c, N, hrep))
    if len(cand_list) > limit:
       worst=0
       for i in range(1,len(cand_list)):
          if cand_list[i][0] > cand_list[worst][0]: worst=i
       cand_list.pop(worst)
    return hrep,c
```
This stores hrep arrays; if later best_h uses same array? We should copy when using as best to avoid modification? evaluate returns new array. Local functions copy before mutating. Good.

`add_top_candidate(top_candidates, N, h, limit=8)`:
```
def add_top_candidate(top_candidates, N, h, limit=8):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size != N: return
    for entry in top_candidates:
       if entry[0]==N:
          if np.allclose(entry[1], h, rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-7): return
    if len(top_candidates) < limit:
       top_candidates.append((N, np.array(h,float,copy=True)))
```

Pseudo:
```
def improve_candidate(h_in, idx, time_budget, seed, deadline):
    n=idx[0]
    h0=repair_sum(h_in)
    if time.time() >= deadline: return h0, objective_c5(h0)
    c0=correlation_array(h0); m0=float(np.max(c0))*2/n
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_val=m0
    bin_like = is_binary_like(h0)
    # binary
    if bin_like and time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h_bin=make_binary(h0) if n%2==0 else None
       if h_bin is not None:
          c_bin=correlation_array(h_bin)
          deadline_bin=min(deadline, time.time()+max(20, 0.35*time_budget))
          hb,cb,mb_count = binary_search(h_bin, c_bin, idx, deadline_bin, seed)
          mb=mb_count*2/n
          if mb < best_val:
              best_val=mb; best_h=np.array(hb,float,copy=True)
          start_h = hb.copy(); start_c = cb.copy()
       else:
          start_h=h0.copy(); start_c=c0.copy()
    else:
       start_h=h0.copy(); start_c=c0.copy()
    # pair descent from start_h if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       deadline_pair = min(deadline, time.time() + max(20, 0.40*(deadline-time.time())))
       hp,cp,mp_count=pair_descent(start_h,start_c,idx,deadline_pair,seed+1000)
       mp=mp_count*2/n
       if mp < best_val:
          best_val=mp; best_h=np.array(hp,float,copy=True)
    # Also pair from h0 if different and time left
    if best_h is not h0 and time.time()<deadline-10:
       deadline2 = min(deadline, time.time()+max(10,0.2*(deadline-time.time())))
       hp2,cp2,mp2=pair_descent(h0.copy(),c0.copy(),idx,deadline2,seed+2000)
       ...
    # SLSQP from best_h
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       deadline_sl=min(deadline, time.time()+max(10, 0.3*(deadline-time.time())))
       hs,ms = slsqp_polish(best_h, idx, deadline_sl, active_tol=0.1, maxiter=30)
       if ms < best_val: best=hs
       if time.time()<deadline-10:
          hs2,ms2=slsqp_polish(best_h, idx, min(deadline,time.time()+...), active_tol=0.5, maxiter=20)
          update
    # final repair best_h
    best_h=repair_sum(best_h)
    return best_h, objective_c5(best_h)
```

Now, let's think about `c` update correctness for diagonal when applying pair move sequential: Suppose pair (i,j). We apply i->x. This updates diagonal i, and off-diag with i including term involving j old. Then apply j->y. This updates diagonal j and off-diag with j including pair (i,j) with h_i=x. The net for pair (i,j): Initial term h_i_old(1-h_j_old). After i step: x(1-h_j_old). After j step: x(1-y). Final correct. Term reverse similarly. Good. Diagonal corrections independent. Sum preserved. Great.

def trim_candidates(cand_list, limit):
   if len(cand_list) <= limit: return
   worst=0
   for i in range(1,len(cand_list)):
      if cand_list[i][0] > cand_list[worst][0]: worst=i
   cand_list.pop(worst)
```

Let's write `improve_candidate` precisely:
```
def improve_candidate(h_in, idx, deadline, seed):
    n = idx[0]
    h0 = repair_sum(h_in)
    if h0.size != n: return h0, objective_c5(h0)
    c0 = correlation_array(h0)
    m0 = float(np.max(c0)) * (2.0/n)
    best_h = h0.copy(); best_c = m0
    # binary phase
    if n % 2 == 0 and is_binary_like(h0) and time.time() < deadline - 20:
       h_bin = make_binary(h0)
       c_bin = correlation_array(h_bin)
       bin_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.4*(deadline-time.time())))
       hb, cb, mb_count = binary_search(h_bin, c_bin, idx, bin_deadline, seed)
       mb = float(mb_count) * (2.0/n)
       if mb < best_c:
          best_c=mb; best_h=np.array(hb,float,copy=True)
       start_h=np.array(hb,float,copy=True); start_c=np.array(cb,float,copy=True); start_m=mb
    else:
       start_h=h0.copy(); start_c=c0.copy(); start_m=m0
    # pair descent from start
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       pair_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.45*(deadline-time.time())))
       hp,cp,mp_count=pair_descent(start_h,start_c,idx,pair_deadline,seed+100)
       mp=float(mp_count)*(2.0/n)
       if mp < best_c:
          best_c=mp; best_h=np.array(hp,float,copy=True)
    # pair descent from original if different? To avoid too long, only if time left and original m < best_c+0.05
    if h0 is not start_h and time.time() < deadline - 10:
       orig_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.25*(deadline-time.time())))
       hp2,cp2,mp2_count=pair_descent(h0.copy(),c0.copy(),idx,orig_deadline,seed+200)
       mp2=float(mp2_count)*(2.0/n)
       if mp2 < best_c:
          best_c=mp2; best_h=np.array(hp2,float,copy=True)
    # SLSQP from best
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       sl_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.3*(deadline-time.time())))
       hs, c5s = slsqp_polish(best_h, sl_deadline, active_tol=0.1, maxiter=30)
       if c5s < best_c:
          best_c=c5s; best_h=np.array(hs,float,copy=True)
       if time.time() < deadline - 10:
          sl_deadline2 = min(deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.2*(deadline-time.time())))
          hs2, c5s2 = slsqp_polish(best_h, sl_deadline2, active_tol=0.5, maxiter=20)
          if c5s2 < best_c:
             best_c=c5s2; best_h=np.array(hs2,float,copy=True)
    best_h = repair_sum(best_h)
    cfinal = objective_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(cfinal)
```

Now, test mentally a simple `apply_change` with h length 2. off=1, J=[0,1]. For i=0 pos=[1,2]? off+J-0=[1,2] length3 indices; neg=off+J? i-J=[0,-1]? [1,0]. c length 3 indices 0,1,2. Suppose h=[0,1]. Changing i=0 to1 delta1: c[pos0] +=1*(1-h) = [0,1,1]? Wait 1-h=[1,0]; c[1]+=1; c[2]+=0. c[neg0] -=1*h = [0,1]; c[1]-=0; c[0]-=1. diag c[off]=c[1] subtract delta^2=1. ```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def repair_sum(h):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(h.size)

    for _ in range(30):
        err = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(err) < 1e-13:
            break
        if err > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        add = err / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        h[mask] += add
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    for _ in range(20):
        err = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(err) < 1e-13:
            break
        if err > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + err)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + err)

    err = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(err) > 1e-9:
        h += err / float(h.size)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    return h


def objective_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    dx = 2.0 / float(h.size)
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(vals) * dx)


def evaluate_candidate(h):
    if h is None:
        return None, 1e100
    try:
        h = repair_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel())
    except Exception:
        return None, 1e100
    if h.size == 0:
        return None, 1e100
    if np.any(h < -1e-8) or np.any(h > 1.0 + 1e-8):
        return h, 1e100
    c5 = objective_c5(h)
    if not np.isfinite(c5):
        return h, 1e100
    return h, float(c5)


def correlation_array(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(h.size)
    off = n - 1
    c = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    om = 1.0 - h
    for d in range(n):
        m = n - d
        c[off + d] = float(np.dot(h[:m], om[d:]))
        c[off - d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], om[:m]))
    return c


def build_idx(n):
    n = int(n)
    J = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    off = n - 1
    pos = [off + (J - i) for i in range(n)]
    neg = [off + (i - J) for i in range(n)]
    return (n, off, J, pos, neg)


def get_idx(n, cache):
    n = int(n)
    if n not in cache:
        cache[n] = build_idx(n)
    return cache[n]


def apply_change(c, h, idx, new, off, pos, neg):
    old = float(h[idx])
    delta = float(new) - old
    if abs(delta) < 1e-16:
        return
    p = pos[idx]
    q = neg[idx]
    c[p] += delta * (1.0 - h)
    c[q] -= delta * h
    c[off] -= delta * delta
    h[idx] = new


def pair_candidates(h, i, j):
    s = float(h[i] + h[j])
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    curr = float(h[i])

    vals = [
        curr,
        0.0,
        1.0,
        lo,
        hi,
        0.5,
        s - 0.5,
        0.5 * s,
    ]
    for d in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5):
        vals.append(curr + d)
        vals.append(curr - d)

    out = []
    seen = set()
    for v in vals:
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            continue
        if v < lo - 1e-9 or v > hi + 1e-9:
            continue
        if v < lo:
            v = lo
        elif v > hi:
            v = hi
        key = round(float(v), 10)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            out.append(float(v))
    return out


def pair_trial(c, h, i, j, xi, yj, off, pos, neg):
    c2 = c.copy()
    old_i = float(h[i])
    old_j = float(h[j])
    apply_change(c2, h, i, xi, off, pos, neg)
    apply_change(c2, h, j, yj, off, pos, neg)
    m = float(np.max(c2))
    h[i] = old_i
    h[j] = old_j
    return m, c2


def pair_descent(h, c, idx, deadline, seed=0, max_passes=60, random_candidates=2):
    n, off, J, pos, neg = idx
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    c = np.array(c, dtype=float, copy=True)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    passes = 0

    while passes < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        perm = rng.permutation(n)
        improved_pass = False

        for t in range(n // 2):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            i = int(perm[2 * t])
            j = int(perm[2 * t + 1])
            s = float(h[i] + h[j])
            lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
            hi = min(1.0, s)
            if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                continue

            vals = pair_candidates(h, i, j)
            if random_candidates > 0:
                for _ in range(random_candidates):
                    vals.append(float(lo + rng.random() * (hi - lo)))

            local-best = cur_m
            local_best_m = cur_m
            local_best_c = None
            local_xi = None
            for xi in vals:
                yj = s - float(xi)
                m, c2 = pair_trial(c, h, i, j, float(xi), float(yj), off, pos, neg)
                if m < local_best_m - 1e-12:
                    local_best_m = m
                    local_best_c = c2
                    local_xi = float(xi)

            if local_best_c is not None:
                c = local_best_c
                h[i] = local_xi
                h[j] = s - local_xi
                cur_m = local_best_m
                improved_pass = True
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()

        passes += 1
        if improved_pass:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if (improved_pass or no_improve >= 2) and time.time() < deadline:
            c = correlation_array(h)
            cur_m = float(np.max(c))
            if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            if cur_m > best_m + 5.0:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                cur_m = best_m

        if no_improve >= 2 and time.time() < deadline:
            jumped = False
            for _ in range(8):
                i = int(rng.integers(n))
                j = int(rng.integers(n))
                if i == j:
                    continue
                s = float(h[i] + h[j])
                lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
                hi = min(1.0, s)
                if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                    continue
                xi = float(lo + rng.random() * (hi - lo))
                yj = s - xi
                m, c2 = pair_trial(c, h, i, j, xi, yj, off, pos, neg)
                if m <= cur_m + 1.0:
                    c = c2
                    h[i] = xi
                    h[j] = yj
                    cur_m = m
                    jumped = True
                    if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                        best_m = cur_m
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()
                    break
            if jumped:
                no_improve = 0
                c = correlation_array(h)
                cur_m = float(np.max(c))
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()

        if no_improve >= 4:
            break

    return best_h, best_c, float(best_m)


def swap_trial(c, h, p, q, off, pos, neg):
    c2 = c.copy()
    oldp = float(h[p])
    oldq = float(h[q])
    apply_change(c2, h, p, 0.0, off, pos, neg)
    apply_change(c2, h, q, 1.0, off, pos, neg)
    m = float(np.max(c2))
    h[p] = oldp
    h[q] = oldq
    return m, c2


def make_binary(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    if n % 2 == 0:
        target = n // 2
    else:
        target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    order = np.argsort(-v)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[order[:target]] = 1.0
    return b


def binary_search(h_in, c_in, idx, deadline, seed=0, full_sample=5000, max_hill_passes=35):
    n, off, J, pos, neg = idx
    h = np.array(h_in, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.where(h > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)) > 1e-10:
        h = make_binary(h)

    c = correlation_array(h)
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    passes = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while passes < max_hill_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break

        total = int(ones.size) * int(zeros.size)
        local_best_m = cur_m
        local_best_c = None
        local_pq = None

        if total <= full_sample:
            for ip in range(ones.size):
                p = int(ones[ip])
                for iq in range(zeros.size):
                    q = int(zeros[iq])
                    m, c2 = swap_trial(c, h, p, q, off, pos, neg)
                    if m < local_best_m - 1e-12:
                        local_best_m = m
                        local_best_c = c2
                        local_pq = (p, q)
        else:
            for _ in range(full_sample):
                p = int(ones[rng.integers(ones.size)])
                q = int(zeros[rng.integers(zeros.size)])
                m, c2 = swap_trial(c, h, p, q, off, pos, neg)
                if m < local_best_m - 1e-12:
                    local_best_m = m
                    local_best_c = c2
                    local_pq = (p, q)

        if local_pq is not None and local_best_m < cur_m - 1e-12:
            p, q = local_pq
            h[p] = 0.0
            h[q] = 1.0
            c = local_best_c
            cur_m = local_best_m
            if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break

        passes += 1
        if passes % 5 == 0 and time.time() < deadline:
            c = correlation_array(h)
            cur_m = float(np.max(c))
            if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            if cur_m > best_m + 5.0:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                cur_m = best_m

    if time.time() < deadline:
        T = 0.8
        min_T = 0.05
        it = 0
        while time.time() < deadline and T > min_T:
            it += 1
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
            zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
            if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
                break
            p = int(ones[rng.integers(ones.size)])
            q = int(zeros[rng.integers(zeros.size)])
            m, c2 = swap_trial(c, h, p, q, off, pos, neg)
            delta = m - cur_m
            if delta <= 1e-12 or (delta < T and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / T)):
                h[p] = 0.0
                h[q] = 1.0
                c = c2
                cur_m = m
                if m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best_m = m
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()

            if it % 20000 == 0:
                if cur_m > best_m + 5.0:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    c = best_c.copy()
                    cur_m = best_m
                    T = max(T, 0.5)
                T = max(min_T, T * 0.99998)
            else:
                T = max(min_T, T * 0.99999)

    return best_h, best_c, float(best_m)


def slsqp_fun(x, n, dx, active_tol):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    c = correlation_array(x)
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def slsqp_jac(x, n, dx, active_tol):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    c = correlation_array(x)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    lags = np.nonzero(c >= m - active_tol)[0]
    if lags.size == 0:
        lags = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    for li in lags:
        d = int(li) - off
        start = max(0, -d)
        end = min(n, n - d)
        if start >= end:
            continue
        first = np.arange(start, end)
        second = first + d
        grad[first] += 1.0 - x[second]
        grad[second] -= x[first]

    grad /= float(lags.size)
    grad *= dx
    return grad


def sum_constraint(x, target):
    return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()) - target)


def slsqp_polish(h_in, deadline, active_tol=0.1, maxiter=30):
    n = int(np.size(h_in))
    h = repair_sum(h_in)
    c5 = objective_c5(h)
    if n <= 0 or time.time() >= deadline:
        return h, float(c5)

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "args": (target,)}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_fun,
            h.copy(),
            args=(n, dx, active_tol),
            jac=slsqp_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            method="SLSQP",
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-10, "disp": False},
        )
        x = repair_sum(res.x)
        c5 = objective_c5(x)
        if np.isfinite(c5):
            return x, float(c5)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return h, float(c5)


def is_binary_like(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return False
    frac = int(np.count_nonzero((h > 1e-7) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-7)))
    return frac <= max(3, int(0.30 * n))


def resample_parent(p, N, linear=True):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.full(int(N), 0.5, dtype=float)
    n_in = int(p.size)
    N = int(N)
    xnew = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / N)
    xold = (np.arange(n_in, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_in)
    if linear:
        v = np.interp(xnew, xold, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    else:
        idx = np.floor(xnew * (0.5 * n_in)).astype(int)
        idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n_in - 1)
        v = p[idx]
    return repair_sum(v)


def random_binary(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)] = 1.0
    return h


def random_continuous(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    h = 0.5 + 0.35 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return repair_sum(h)


def sine_seed(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    freqs = (0.55, 0.70, 0.80, 0.95, 1.15, 1.35)
    freq = float(freqs[int(rng.integers(len(freqs)))])
    phase = 2.0 * math.pi * float(rng.random())
    amp = 0.30 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + phase)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return repair_sum(h)


def add_candidate(cand_list, hcand, N, limit=16):
    hrep, c = evaluate_candidate(hcand)
    if hrep is None:
        return None, 1e100
    cand_list.append((c, int(N), hrep))
    if len(cand_list) > limit:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
            if cand_list[i][0] > cand_list[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        cand_list.pop(worst)
    return hrep, c


def trim_candidates(cand_list, limit=16):
    if len(cand_list) <= limit:
        return
    worst = 0
    for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
        if cand_list[i][0] > cand_list[worst][0]:
            worst = i
    cand_list.pop(worst)


def extract_best(cand_list):
    if not cand_list:
        return None
    bi = 0
    for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
        if cand_list[i][0] < cand_list[bi][0]:
            bi = i
    return cand_list.pop(bi)


def add_top_candidate(top_candidates, N, h, limit=8):
    N = int(N)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size != N or h.size == 0:
        return
    for entry in top_candidates:
        if entry[0] == N:
            if np.allclose(entry[1], h, rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-7):
                return
    if len(top_candidates) < limit:
        top_candidates.append((N, h.copy()))


def improve_candidate(h_in, idx, deadline, seed):
    n, off, J, pos, neg = idx
    h0 = repair_sum(h_in)
    if h0.size != n:
        return h0, objective_c5(h0)

    c0 = correlation_array(h0)
    m0 = float(np.max(c0)) * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = m0
    used_binary = False

    if n % 2 == 0 and is_binary_like(h0) and time.time() < deadline - 20:
        h_bin = make_binary(h0)
        c_bin = correlation_array(h_bin)
        bin_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.40 * (deadline - time.time())))
        hb, cb, mb_count = binary_search(h_bin, c_bin, idx, bin_deadline, seed)
        mb = float(mb_count) * (2.0 / n)
        if mb < best_c:
            best_c = mb
            best_h = np.array(hb, dtype=float, copy=True)
        start_h = np.array(hb, dtype=float, copy=True)
        start_c = np.array(cb, dtype=float, copy=True)
        used_binary = True
    else:
        start_h = h0.copy()
        start_c = c0.copy()

    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        pd = min(deadline, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.45 * (deadline - time.time())))
        hp, cp, mp_count = pair_descent(start_h, start_c, idx, pd, seed + 100)
        mp = float(mp_count) * (2.0 / n)
        if mp < best_c:
            best_c = mp
            best_h = np.array(hp, dtype=float, copy=True)

    if used_binary and m0 < best_c + 0.05 and time.time() < deadline - 10:
        pd2 = min(deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.25 * (deadline - time.time())))
        hp2, cp2, mp2_count = pair_descent(h0.copy(), c0.copy(), idx, pd2, seed + 500)
        mp2 = float(mp2_count) * (2.0 / n)
        if mp2 < best_c:
            best_c = mp2
            best_h = np.array(hp2, dtype=float, copy=True)

    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        smd = min(deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.30 * (deadline - time.time())))
        hs, c5s = slsqp_polish(best_h, smd, active_tol=0.10, maxiter=30)
        if c5s < best_c:
            best_c = c5s
            best_h = np.array(hs, dtype=float, copy=True)

        if time.time() < deadline - 10:
            smd2 = min(deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.20 * (deadline - time.time())))
            hs2, c5s2 = slsqp_polish(best_h, smd2, active_tol=0.50, maxiter=20)
            if c5s2 < best_c:
                best_c = c5s2
                best_h = np.array(hs2, dtype=float, copy=True)

    best_h = repair_sum(best_h)
    best_c = objective_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 30.0:
        budget = 30.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 20.0)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100
    cand_list = []
    idx_cache = {}

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        hrep, c = evaluate_candidate(parent)
        if hrep is not None and c < best_c:
            best_h = hrep.copy()
            best_c = c
        cand_list.append((c, int(parent.size), hrep))
        trim_candidates(cand_list, 18)

    Ns = []

    def _add_N(N):
        N = int(N)
        if 32 <= N <= 200:
            for x in Ns:
                if x == N:
                    return
            Ns.append(N)

    if parent is not None:
        Npar = int(parent.size)
        if Npar % 2 == 0:
            _add_N(Npar)
        else:
            _add_N(Npar + 1)

    for N in (100, 104, 112, 120, 128, 96, 102, 108, 116, 160):
        _add_N(N)
        if len(Ns) >= 10:
            break

    for N in Ns:
        if time.time() > deadline - 25:
            break
        if parent is not None:
            hseed = resample_parent(parent, N, True)
            hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
            if c < best_c:
                best_h = hrep.copy()
                best_c = c

            hseed = resample_parent(parent, N, False)
            hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
            if c < best_c:
                best_h = hrep.copy()
                best_c = c

            hseed = make_binary(resample_parent(parent, N, True))
            hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
            if c < best_c:
                best_h = hrep.copy()
                best_c = c

        hseed = random_binary(N, seed + 1000 + int(N) * 17)
        hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = hrep.copy()
            best_c = c

        if N <= 128:
            hseed = random_continuous(N, seed + 5000 + int(N) * 13)
            hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
            if c < best_c:
                best_h = hrep.copy()
                best_c = c

        hseed = sine_seed(N, seed + 9000 + int(N) * 11)
        hrep, c = add_candidate(cand_list, hseed, N, 18)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = hrep.copy()
            best_c = c

        trim_candidates(cand_list, 18)

    top_candidates = []
    temp = []
    for e in cand_list:
        temp.append(e)
    for _ in range(3):
        e = extract_best(temp)
        if e is None:
            break
        add_top_candidate(top_candidates, e[1], e[2], 8)

    if parent is not None:
        for Nforce in (100, 128, 112):
            if time.time() > deadline - 100:
                break
            try:
                hforce = make_binary(resample_parent(parent, Nforce, True))
                add_top_candidate(top_candidates, Nforce, hforce, 8)
            except Exception:
                pass

    add_top_candidate(top_candidates, 100, random_binary(100, seed + 123456), 8)
    add_top_candidate(top_candidates, 128, random_binary(128, seed + 654321), 8)

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            add_top_candidate(top_candidates, 100, resample_parent(parent, 100, True), 8)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not top_candidates:
        add_top_candidate(top_candidates, 100, np.full(100, 0.5, dtype=float), 8)

    priority = (260, 210, 170, 140, 115, 90, 70, 55)
    for i, item in enumerate(top_candidates):
        if time.time() > deadline - 25:
            break
        N, hnd = item
        alloc = priority[i] if i < len(priority) else 45
        rem = deadline - time.time() - 20.0
        if alloc > rem:
            alloc = rem
        if alloc < 15:
            break
        idx = get_idx(N, idx_cache)
        cdeadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(1.0, alloc))
        try:
            h2, c2 = improve_candidate(hnd, idx, cdeadline, seed + 20000 + i * 31 + N)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = np.array(h2, dtype=float, copy=True)
                best_c = c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 20:
        Nbest = int(best_h.size)
        idx = get_idx(Nbest, idx_cache)
        try:
            cstart = correlation_array(best_h)
            pd = min(deadline, time.time() + max(8.0, min(35.0, deadline - time.time())))
            hp, cp, mp = pair_descent(best_h, cstart, idx, pd, seed + 777777)
            hp, c5 = evaluate_candidate(hp)
            if c5 < best_c:
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_c = c5
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 15:
        try:
            smd = min(deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, min(30.0, deadline - time.time())))
            hs, c5s = slsqp_polish(best_h, smd, active_tol=0.25, maxiter=20)
            if c5s < best_c:
                best_h = np.array(hs, dtype=float, copy=True)
                best_c = c5s
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = repair_sum(np.full(100, 0.5, dtype=float))
        best_c = objective_c5(best_h)

    best_h = repair_sum(best_h)
    best_c = float(objective_c5(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_h.size)
```
